## Status and evidence rules

- `closed` can retain an explicit bounded residual when the accepted test receipt names the item and its profile. It does not claim universal closure.
- `partial` records either an explicit incomplete property or a specific absence of item-bound accepted evidence; each row says which. `UNRUN` is not a pass and does not mean environmental red.
- Source-card, package-level acceptance and triage records alone do not close a finding. The 71 E02 package receipts are retained as bundle-level context but their finding-level behavioral verdict is UNRUN; each row cites the source-card discriminator and record-inspection probe. LA-021 cites the separate reviewed bounded probe.
- B73 remains partial: the expected-red clean-782 probe reproduced the legacy-hook split (`right=2` persisted while `completed_nodes=["left"]`). Commit `bac6996` fences the unsafe node-only fallback, with bounded 11/11 focused and 9/9 whole-file evidence. Four-base replay, wider fail-fast/continue durable-head behavior, and arbitrary custom-hook persistence semantics remain UNRUN.

| LA-025 | SCL-03 | partial | partial | — | a: Resolve a retained v1 snapshot reference from the existing CAS and prove the cache hit reads those exact bytes without network access. A same-URL v2 snapshot is a distinct reference and must not replace v1; if the blob is gone or access is denied, return a typed gap or an explicit refresh with new lineage. Preserve source identity and prove the behavior through the existing consumer. | Production empty-seed search→enrich already carries content-addressed bytes: the API persists the WebEvidenceBundle, resolves the selected source to `SourceSpec(kind=bytes)`, reads CAS content by ArtifactID and verifies digest/size; `_acquire_bytes` verifies again without URL re-fetch. The remaining defect is source-metadata binding. `build_source_metadata` copies content digest/type but omits the same FetchResult’s ArtifactRef/artifact_id, byte_size, final_url, redirect_chain, ETag, Last-Modified, license, fetch_profile, lineage_parent_artifact_id and refresh_reason. SourceMetadata derives only an ArtifactID from the digest; the other fields stay at defaults. `_source_spec_from_snapshot` fills missing artifact_id, final_url, redirect_chain, ETag, Last-Modified, fetch_profile, refresh lineage, byte_size and license from `cache.get(source_url)`, which is the URL’s current record rather than the retained source snapshot. A retained v1 can therefore keep v1 bytes while inheriting same-URL v2 metadata; if v2 byte_size differs, the API rejects the valid v1 bytes. The handoff also reduces full ArtifactRef to ArtifactID, losing kind/media_type/manifest_profile_sha256. The live fetcher leaves effective fetch_profile at its default `{}`; DocSourceSpec/DocMeta do not persist fetch profile or refresh lineage, and WebEvidenceBundle retains only fields its producer supplied. Residual: bind ArtifactRef, digest, final URL/redirects/headers/license/size/effective fetch profile/refresh lineage to the same FetchResult through the persisted consumer. Prove v1 no-egress replay, same-URL v2 isolation and lineage, current-cache mutation does not contaminate/refuse v1, plus typed missing/access failure. Keep partial; behavioral and four-base evidence remain UNRUN. | implementation_owner: Scholar search metadata producer and Scholar API snapshot-enrichment bridge owners (roles identified by source path; no individual appointment is claimed) | not_measured: Appendix C partial status retained. Machine manifest: policy-engine/docs/plans/active/agent-packages/PolicyOS_E02R2/raw/p41-custom-20260929T192114Z-396/results.json@sha256:07c7e7b753424d5dee2544cc60f3bed907d88e104ff905d12eaac5ef8e0b38c3.

**Bounded direct evidence.** The direct Pareto JUnit is 35/35 and the importer JUnit is 53/53. A separate finite-input permutation probe passes 6/6 (three cases under two input orders), with finite references and hypervolume. These receipts support only their tested owner/consumer cases. The finite-hypervolume overflow falsifier uses finite ±1e308 inputs and finds `compute_front` raises validation on a non-finite derived hypervolume instead of returning a typed limited/unavailable result. The direct champion-selector falsifier changes the selected winner with input order: `b111_finite`, `b111_nan`, or `b111_pos_inf` can win when first. It is a direct consumer probe; production upstream emission of non-finite inputs, persistence/CAS reachability, and N9 promotion/publication are `not_established`.

**P37/P38 limit and independent producer gap.** The DTO recomputes equality/uniqueness of the supplied sets. In the ordinary `PolicyArtifactBuilder._build_frontier_report` path, however, `source_feasible_candidate_hashes` is copied from `projection.eligible_candidate_hashes` and `eligibility_unknown_candidate_hashes` remains empty. The ordinary producer has no independent source-feasible or unknown-eligibility input; validation of those caller-provided fields cannot establish the source denominator. Under P37, equality of supplied sets is `recomputed`, while upstream candidate completeness and absence of unknown eligibility are `not_established`. Under P38, the divergent case is a source-feasible candidate omitted before projection: it is absent from both self-derived sets, so equality remains green. Do not describe this path as independently reconciled to an observed source denominator.

The smallest closure is an independent owner-produced source-feasible identity set plus an explicit unknown-eligibility set, bridged into the ordinary report builder, with a semantic negative test for an eligible candidate omitted before projection and a separate explicit-unknown control. Current capability labels: `producer_missing`, `bridge_missing`, `semantic_test_missing`, and `surface_missing`. B111 remains `partial`. The direct champion probe does not establish production non-finite emission. Next steps also include a typed finite-hypervolume overflow outcome, production producer→consumer/persistence evidence, and the complete 21-file P41 matrix.
